```python
    return s[::-1]
```
`[::-1]` slices with a step of -1, so it walks the string backwards.

user: write a function that reverses a string in python
assistant: ```python
def reverse(s):
    return s[::-1]
```
`[::-1]` slices with a step of -1, so it walks the string backwards.

user: one more: how do i make a http request in python
assistant: ```python
import urllib.request
with urllib.request.urlopen(url) as r:
    body = r.read()
```
Or `pip install requests` and use `requests.get(url)`.

user: how do i make a http request in python
assistant: ```python
import urllib.request
with urllib.request.urlopen(url) as r:
    body = r.read()
```

user: write a function that reverses a string in python
assistant: ```python
def reverse(s):
    return s[::-1]
```
`[::-1]` slices with a step of -1, so it walks the string backwards.

```python
def reverse(s):
```

user: quick q: write a function that reverses a string in python
assistant: ```python
def reverse(s):
    return s[::-1]
```
`[::-1]` slices with a step of -1, so it walks the string backwards.

user: hey, write a function that reverses a string in python
assistant: ```python
def reverse(s):
    return s[::-1]
```
`[::-1]` slices with a step of -1, so it walks the string backwards.

```python
    if i % 15 == 0:
        print('FizzBuzz')
    elif i % 3 == 0:
        print('Fizz')
    elif i % 5 == 0:
        print('Buzz')
    else:
        print(i)
```

user: write a function that reverses a string in python
assistant: ```python
def reverse(s):
    return s[::-1]
```
`[::-1]` slices with a step of -1, so it walks the string backwards.

user: how do i make a http request in python

```python
import urllib.request

```

user: one more: write a function that reverses a string in python

```python
def reverse(s):

```
